- **gitnexus-knowledge-graph**: MCP-native knowledge graph engine for AI coding agents. Indexes entire repos into a structured graph (functions, classes, imports, call chains, execution flows) using Tree-sitter ASTs and exposes it via 7 MCP tools. Gives Claude Code, Cursor, and other agents full codebase structural awareness so they stop missing dependencies and breaking call chains. Use when vibe coding large projects, refactoring, understanding unfamiliar codebases, or before making changes that could have cascading effects. ([spoonyc/claude-science-skills](https://github.com/spoonyc/claude-science-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gitnexus-knowledge-graph))

- **gitnexus-repo-map**: Use GitNexus as a lightweight repo-exploration tool to read unfamiliar codebases, generate architecture/module/dependency maps, and inspect functional clusters. CLI-first; web UI is opt-in. Trigger phrases include: "GitNexus / repo map / visual code graph / architecture topology / read this open-source repo / 仓库架构 / 代码拓扑 / 模块地图 / clusters / 读开源项目".
